Title: Histoire des Vaudois, divisee en trois parties: the second part, Histoire des Chrestiens Albigeois, contenant les longues guerres, persecutions qu'ils ont souffert a cause de la doctrine de l'Evangile; and the third part, in two books, contenant la doctrine et discipline laquelle ils ont eu commune entre eux (the Vaudois treatises and the Discipline printed in the old Vaudois tongue with French facing) - the Geneva 1618 printing, Parts II and III only in this scan
Creator: Jean Paul Perrin, pastor of the Reformed church at Nyons in Dauphine (the host catalogue names him; the Part II title page in the OCR names no author, and the Part III headings carry the running title of his history)
Publisher: Geneve: pour Matthieu Berjon (imprint read in the OCR on the Part II title page, file line 17)
Publication Date: 1618 (host catalogue; the Part II title page in the OCR carries the imprint but its year is not legible)
Rights: Public Domain (an early seventeenth-century print carrying a Creative Commons Public Domain Mark 1.0 on the host item; public domain by date under the project's 95-year rule)
Language: fra
Source: Internet Archive item bub_gb_x_0dyBHt4kcC, file bub_gb_x_0dyBHt4kcC_djvu.txt (a Google Books scan of the National Central Library of Rome copy; collection europeanlibraries), fetched directly (curl, raw bytes) by the library/source-research thread (Era VII session, second-pass agent for the continental Reformed and Scandinavian worlds), 2026-10-23; host rights statement: licenseurl http://creativecommons.org/publicdomain/mark/1.0/ (Public Domain Mark); not access-restricted.
Provenance note: Header prepended; the OCR text follows unchanged (no characters altered; the raw file contains 0 long-s glyphs, so no long-s normalisation was done). Rights basis restated: an early seventeenth-century print carrying a Creative Commons Public Domain Mark 1.0 on the host item; public domain by date under the project's 95-year rule.
Content note: This scan holds Parts II and III of Perrin's three-part Histoire des Vaudois, not Part I: it opens on the title page of the Histoire des Chrestiens Albigeois (file line 1) and runs through that history in its books (running heads 'Albigeois. Liure I.' and so on), then TROISIEME PARTIE DE L'HISTOIRE DES Vaudois et Albigeois, Liure I, 'contenant la Doctrine et Discipline laquelle ils ont eu commune entr'eux' (file line 7317) and LIVRE SECOND DE LA TROISIESME PARTIE, 'contenant la Discipline sous laquelle ils ont vescu' (file line 11169), where the old Vaudois texts are printed in their own tongue with Perrin's French beside them in parallel columns ('La Disciplina conten en si tot a doctrina' / 'La Discipline contient en soy toute ...', from line 11176). Part I (the origin, belief and persecutions of the Vaudois, with the 1532 articles) is vendored from the 1619 printing as perrin_histoire-des-vaudois-fra_1619.txt; the 1624 English of all three parts, reprinted in 1847, is vendored as perrin_history-ancient-christians-valleys-alps_1847.txt, so this file is the French original beside that English for Parts II and III. The Part II claim that the Albigenses were Waldenses is Perrin's own and is not borne out by the sources (see the first-pass dossier). OCR sampled at 10/30/50/70/90 percent (about 0.64 of tokens read as words against a clean modern-French baseline of 0.92; the samples at 50, 70 and 90 percent fall in the parallel-column section, where the old Vaudois tongue is not in the baseline and the two columns are interleaved by the OCR): the 1618 typography's long s is read as f, u and v are interchanged as printed, and column interleaving breaks sentences in Part III. No U+017F characters were present. SECOND WITNESS for exact wording (INTAKE scan-quality rule): locate a passage here, then verify against the page image; for the Vaudois-tongue texts the page image is the only reliable reading.
